# Current classroom image credits

544 approved images: 42 portraits and 502 artworks. Revised 19 November 2026. Teacher-requested exclusions have been removed from current publication.

## Zebra Farm Bus
- File: assets/featured-american/01-zebra-farm-bus.webp
- Credit: Jonathan Green · © Jonathan Green; The Fralin Museum of Art, University of Virginia, Gift of Ellen A. Hennessy,2010.14.10; museum-credited reproduction published by Crozet Gazette
- Rights: Copyright retained — Canadian fair dealing (education/criticism)
- Source: https://www.crozetgazette.com/2025/05/02/free-union-school-students-win-uva-contest/
- Changes: Resized to a maximum 640pixels and converted to WebP; complete composition retained, no artistic alterations.
- licenseUrl: https://laws-lois.justice.gc.ca/eng/acts/C-42/section-29.html
- rightsBasis: Canadian Copyright Act sections29 and29.1: limited teacher-curated Grade7/8 education and criticism, with creator/source acknowledgement. Copyright and reproduction rights retained; no open licence, permission or automatic section30.04 applicability claimed.
- rightsAssessment: Purpose: authentic classroom education and work-specific visual criticism; this selected-work feature identifies the work that students study, with original analysis adjacent to the image. Character: the image appears as a small artist-card preview and profile study image on public GitHub Pages and in its public repository; access by visitors beyond the class and persistent public copying weigh against fairness. Amount: one complete low-resolution composition is necessary to analyse this particular artwork and recognize its significance; the file is capped at 640pixels, with no high-resolution version supplied. Alternatives: a different artwork would not serve criticism of this canonical work; text alone cannot show its formal structure, while a restricted classroom host would reduce dissemination. Nature: previously lawfully published artwork reproduced on a public museum, artist or museum-credited publication. Effect (market): the small study file and substantial educational context reduce substitution for prints/books/high-quality reproductions, but a licensing market may exist and remains a countervailing consideration. This is a contextual assessment, not a declaration that public educational copying is automatically fair.
- description: A zebra-striped bus carries a row of people through an intensely coloured coastal landscape beneath a setting sun. The decorated vehicle and repeated passengers connect everyday travel with Green’s celebration of Gullah community life.
- Information sources: https://uvafralinartmuseum.virginia.edu/system/files/WE24%20Self-Guide.pdf · https://www.jonathangreenstudios.com/about

## Folk Family

## Landscape drawing for Santa Maria della Neve
- File: assets/school-reviewed/03-landscape-drawing-for-santa-maria-della-neve.webp
- Credit: Leonardo da Vinci · Public-domain artwork; study reproduction published by WikiArt; reproduction photographer not identified.
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://www.wikiart.org/en/leonardo-da-vinci/landscape-drawing-for-santa-maria-della-neve-1473
- Changes: Reduced to at most 640px and converted to WebP; complete composition preserved.
- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/
- rightsBasis: Underlying artwork copyright expired; faithful reduced reproduction of a public-domain work.
- description: Cliffs, a river valley and distant buildings unfold through fine pen lines, while curved marks suggest the movement of water and the shapes of plants. Compare the dense foreground with the lighter distance: how does Leonardo build a sense of space using lines rather than painted colour?
- Information sources: https://www.wikiart.org/en/leonardo-da-vinci/landscape-drawing-for-santa-maria-della-neve-1473

- description: This close portrait places a young woman before a dark juniper tree and a distant watery landscape. Her pale face stands out against the dense foliage, while carefully described curls and clothing bring attention to small differences in texture.

- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Leonardo da Vinci; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.

## Portrait of an Unknown Woman (La Belle Ferroniere)
- File: assets/school-reviewed/03-portrait-of-an-unknown-woman-la-belle-ferroniere.webp

## The Last Supper
- File: assets/gallery-classic/03-03.webp

- Rights: Public domain

- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Leonardo da Vinci; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: Jesus sits at the centre of a long table as the disciples respond with different gestures and expressions. The ceiling, walls and windows lead the eye toward him, while the figures form smaller groups that make the large gathering easier to read.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:The_Last_Supper_-_Leonardo_Da_Vinci_-_High_Resolution_32x16.jpg

- File: assets/school-reviewed/03-isabella-d-este.webp

- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/
- rightsBasis: Underlying artwork copyright expired; faithful reduced reproduction of a public-domain work.

## Mona Lisa
- File: assets/03-artwork.webp
- Credit: Leonardo da Vinci · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Mona_Lisa,_by_Leonardo_da_Vinci,_from_C2RMF_retouched.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP. Responsive display may crop decorative card thumbnails.
- description: A seated woman turns toward us with her hands folded, while winding paths and distant mountains stretch behind her. Soft transitions between light and shadow make the face appear rounded without a hard outline; the steady pose contrasts with the complicated landscape.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Mona_Lisa,_by_Leonardo_da_Vinci,_from_C2RMF_retouched.jpg · https://www.louvre.fr/en/explore/the-palace/from-the-mona-lisa-to-the-wedding-feast-at-cana

## Portrait of Pope Julius II
- File: assets/school-reviewed/04-portrait-of-pope-julius-ii.webp

- Rights: Public domain

- rightsBasis: Underlying artwork copyright expired; faithful reduced reproduction of a public-domain work.

- description: A girl holds an umbrella while a group of visitors photographs the mural on a New Orleans wall. How can an object meant to protect someone become an ambiguous symbol when a street artwork is read in its local context?
- Information sources: https://www.flickr.com/photos/infrogmation/2817595676/

## Man with a Blue Cap
- File: assets/gallery-classic/06-04.webp
- Credit: Paul Cezanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_der-mann-mit-der-blauen-muetze.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Paul Cézanne; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: A bearded man in a blue cap looks sideways against a subdued background. Broad areas of light and dark shape the face and coat, giving the portrait weight without describing every fold or hair separately.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_der-mann-mit-der-blauen-muetze.jpg

## House of Dr Gachet at Auvers
- File: assets/gallery-classic/06-08.webp
- Credit: Paul Cézanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_haus-des-dr-gachet-in-auvers.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Paul Cézanne; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: A road bends uphill toward houses beneath a cloudy sky. Angled roofs, walls and stretches of road organise the view into interlocking shapes, allowing a small village scene to become a study of structure as well as place.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_haus-des-dr-gachet-in-auvers.jpg

## The House of the Hanged Man
- File: assets/gallery-classic/06-02.webp
- Credit: Paul Cezanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_das-haus-des-gehenkten.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Paul Cézanne; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: Closely packed houses stand beside a steep village lane and bare trees. Thick-looking patches of colour give the buildings and ground a solid structure, while the narrow route pulls the eye into the clustered forms.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_das-haus-des-gehenkten.jpg

## Mont Sainte-Victoire seen from Bellevue
- File: assets/gallery-classic/06-10.webp
- Credit: Paul Cézanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Paul_Cezanne_La_Montagne_Saint_Victoire_Barnes.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Paul Cézanne; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: A pale mountain rises beyond a patchwork of fields, trees and houses. Changes between warm and cool colour help separate the layers of land, while the mountain’s broad outline holds the detailed foreground together.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Paul_Cezanne_La_Montagne_Saint_Victoire_Barnes.jpg

## The Basket of Apples
- File: assets/06-artwork.webp
- Credit: Paul Cézanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Paul_C%C3%A9zanne_-_The_Basket_of_Apples_-_1926.252_-_Art_Institute_of_Chicago.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP. Responsive display may crop decorative card thumbnails.
- description: Apples spill from a tilted basket beside a bottle, pastries and a rumpled white cloth. The tabletop edges do not quite align, so the arrangement combines recognisable objects with an unstable space that rewards looking beyond ordinary realism.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Paul_C%C3%A9zanne_-_The_Basket_of_Apples_-_1926.252_-_Art_Institute_of_Chicago.jpg

## Portrait of Ambroise Vollard
- File: assets/gallery-classic/06-06.webp
- Credit: Paul Cézanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_portraet-ambroise-vollard.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Paul Cézanne; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: A seated man in a brown suit rests his hands in his lap against an interior made from muted colour patches. The strong vertical body and joined hands make a stable structure, while the uneven surface keeps the portrait visibly connected to the act of painting.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_portraet-ambroise-vollard.jpg

## Apples and Oranges
- File: assets/school-reviewed/06-apples-and-oranges.webp
- Credit: Paul Cezanne · Public-domain artwork; study reproduction published by WikiArt; reproduction photographer not identified.
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://www.wikiart.org/en/paul-cezanne/apples-and-oranges
- Changes: Reduced to at most 640px and converted to WebP; complete composition preserved.
- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/
- rightsBasis: Underlying artwork copyright expired; faithful reduced reproduction of a public-domain work.
- description: Apples and oranges are arranged among folded white cloths, plates and a patterned jug. The tilted tabletop and differently angled objects make the scene feel both solid and unstable. How do colour, repeated round shapes and shifting viewpoints challenge the idea of a still life as a simple copy of what an artist sees?
- Information sources: https://www.wikiart.org/en/paul-cezanne/apples-and-oranges

## The Gardener Vallier
- File: assets/gallery-classic/06-03.webp
- Credit: Paul Cezanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_der-gaertner-vallier.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Paul Cézanne; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: A gardener in a hat sits among green foliage, with his body built from distinct patches of colour. Clothing, face and plants share similar visible marks, connecting the person to the surrounding landscape instead of isolating him against it.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_der-gaertner-vallier.jpg

## Flowers in a Vase
- File: assets/gallery-classic/06-05.webp
- Credit: Paul Cezanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_blumen-in-einer-vase.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Paul Cézanne; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: A dense bouquet rises from a tall blue-green vase on a tabletop. The flowers are organised through clusters of colour and changes in light, while the narrow vase provides a stable vertical support for the spreading arrangement.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_blumen-in-einer-vase.jpg

## Still Life with Teapot
- File: assets/gallery-classic/06-07.webp
- Credit: Paul Cézanne · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_stillleben-mit-teekanne.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; whole artwork or source photograph retained without artistic alteration.
- rightsBasis: Historical artwork by Paul Cézanne; underlying work is public domain. The source record supplies the image’s public-domain designation or photograph licence.
- description: Fruit, pale vessels and a teapot sit beside folds of patterned fabric. Curves in the pots and fruit repeat across the arrangement, while the rising cloth and tilted surfaces make the objects feel more complex than a simple row on a table.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Cezanne_stillleben-mit-teekanne.jpg

- description: A pink triangle is filled and surrounded by many outlined figures covering their eyes, ears or mouths. How does repeating these gestures turn a familiar symbol of silence into a public call to respond to the AIDS crisis?
- Information sources: https://www.haring.com/!/art-work/25

- Source: https://www.wikiart.org/en/henri-rousseau/the-football-players-1908
- Changes: Reduced to at most 640px and converted to WebP; complete composition preserved.
- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/
- rightsBasis: Underlying artwork copyright expired; faithful reduced reproduction of a public-domain work.
- description: Four players in striped outfits move across an open space with a ball, surrounded by a neat fence and trees. Their raised arms and floating-looking poses make the game feel playful. How do repeated stripes and similar gestures connect the figures while still suggesting movement?
- Information sources: https://www.wikiart.org/en/henri-rousseau/the-football-players-1908

## Watchdog
- File: assets/featured-american/13-watchdog.webp
- Credit: George Rodrigue · © George Godfrey Rodrigue, Jr. Family Trust; Private Collection; reproduction published by Ogden Museum of Southern Art
- Rights: Copyright retained — Canadian fair dealing (education/criticism)
- Source: https://ogdenmuseum.org/exhibition/the-first-blue-dog/
- Changes: Resized to a maximum 640pixels and converted to WebP; complete composition retained, no artistic alterations.
- licenseUrl: https://laws-lois.justice.gc.ca/eng/acts/C-42/section-29.html
- rightsBasis: Canadian Copyright Act sections29 and29.1: limited teacher-curated Grade7/8 education and criticism, with creator/source acknowledgement. Copyright and reproduction rights retained; no open licence, permission or automatic section30.04 applicability claimed.
- rightsAssessment: Purpose: authentic classroom education and work-specific visual criticism; this selected-work feature identifies the work that students study, with original analysis adjacent to the image. Character: the image appears as a small artist-card preview and profile study image on public GitHub Pages and in its public repository; access by visitors beyond the class and persistent public copying weigh against fairness. Amount: one complete low-resolution composition is necessary to analyse this particular artwork and recognize its significance; the file is capped at 640pixels, with no high-resolution version supplied. Alternatives: a different artwork would not serve criticism of this canonical work; text alone cannot show its formal structure, while a restricted classroom host would reduce dissemination. Nature: previously lawfully published artwork reproduced on a public museum, artist or museum-credited publication. Effect (market): the small study file and substantial educational context reduce substitution for prints/books/high-quality reproductions, but a licensing market may exist and remains a countervailing consideration. This is a contextual assessment, not a declaration that public educational copying is automatically fair.
- description: A blue-grey dog faces the viewer before a red house in a dark, cloud-filled setting. As the first Blue Dog painting, Watchdog links Rodrigue’s later famous canine image to his earlier illustrations of Cajun folklore.
- Information sources: https://ogdenmuseum.org/exhibition/the-first-blue-dog/ · https://georgerodrigue.com/blog/before_the_blue_dog/

## War Canoes, Alert Bay
- File: assets/gallery-american/14-war-canoes-alert-bay.webp
- Credit: Emily Carr · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily-carr-war-canoes-alert-bay-1912.png

- rightsBasis: Source file records the artwork/reproduction as Public domain. Underlying artist: Emily Carr. No copyright claim is inferred solely from a licence on a new photograph.
- description: Large canoes rest near a coastal settlement, their curved sides occupying much of the foreground. Bands of vivid colour and strong outlines give the boats a weighty presence while keeping the surrounding scene deliberately simplified.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily-carr-war-canoes-alert-bay-1912.png

## Kitwancool
- File: assets/gallery-american/14-kitwancool.webp
- Credit: Emily Carr · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily_Carr_1928_Kitwancool.png
- Changes: Resized to fit 900 pixels and converted to WebP; no artistic alterations.
- licenseUrl: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Commons:Public_domain
- rightsBasis: Source file declares underlying artwork and reproduction public domain; see its stated term/publication basis.
- description: Carved poles rise beside buildings and tree trunks in a closely organized village scene. Carr emphasizes repeated vertical shapes, but differences in colour and carved silhouettes keep the individual poles distinct.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily_Carr_1928_Kitwancool.png

## Church at Yuquot Village (formerly The Indian Church)
- File: assets/gallery-american/14-church-at-yuquot-village-formerly-the-indian-church.webp
- Credit: Emily Carr · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily_Carr_-_Indian_Church.jpg
- Changes: Resized to fit 900 pixels and converted to WebP; no artistic alterations.
- licenseUrl: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Commons:Public_domain
- rightsBasis: Source file records the artwork/reproduction as Public domain. Underlying artist: Emily Carr. No copyright claim is inferred solely from a licence on a new photograph.
- description: A small white church stands against dense, towering green trees. The striking difference in scale makes the building seem enclosed by the forest, creating a visual tension between human construction and the living landscape.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily_Carr_-_Indian_Church.jpg · https://ago.ca/agoinsider/art-toronto-ago-collection

## Blunden Harbour
- File: assets/gallery-american/14-blunden-harbour.webp
- Credit: Emily Carr · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily-carr-blunden-harbour-1930.png
- Changes: Resized to fit 900 pixels and converted to WebP; no artistic alterations.
- licenseUrl: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Commons:Public_domain
- rightsBasis: Source file records the artwork/reproduction as Public domain. Underlying artist: Emily Carr. No copyright claim is inferred solely from a licence on a new photograph.

- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily-carr-blunden-harbour-1930.png

## Forest, British Columbia
- File: assets/gallery-american/14-forest-british-columbia.webp
- Credit: Emily Carr · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily_Carr_(1931%E2%80%9332)_Forest,_British_Columbia.jpg
- Changes: Resized to fit 900 pixels and converted to WebP; no artistic alterations.
- licenseUrl: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Commons:Public_domain
- rightsBasis: Source file records the artwork/reproduction as Public domain. Underlying artist: Emily Carr. No copyright claim is inferred solely from a licence on a new photograph.
- description: Thick trunks and dark foliage create an enclosed forest space with little open sky. Carr’s curving forms turn trees into an interlocking structure, drawing attention to the physical density of the woods.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Emily_Carr_(1931%E2%80%9332)_Forest,_British_Columbia.jpg

## Scorned as Timber, Beloved of the Sky
- File: assets/extra/14-scorned-timber.webp
- Credit: Emily Carr · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Carr,Emily_-_Scorned_as_timber.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; content not cropped or altered.
- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/
- rightsBasis: Historical Canadian artwork; Commons designates the underlying artwork and reproduction public domain. Source file documents the rights basis.
- description: A tall, slender tree rises far above the surrounding landscape into a sweeping sky. Its isolation and small crown make it appear vulnerable yet persistent, while curved cloud bands give the open space a sense of movement.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Carr,Emily_-_Scorned_as_timber.jpg

## Deep Woods
- File: assets/gallery-american/14-deep-woods.webp
- Credit: Emily Carr · Public domain
- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Deep_Woods_by_Emily_Carr,_1936,_oil_on_paper.jpg
- Changes: Resized to fit 900 pixels and converted to WebP; no artistic alterations.
- licenseUrl: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Commons:Public_domain
- rightsBasis: Source file declares underlying artwork and reproduction public domain; see its stated term/publication basis.
- description: Tall, closely spaced trunks lead into a dark green forest interior. Small changes of light and colour create depth, while quick, directional marks keep the scene from feeling still or mechanically ordered.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Deep_Woods_by_Emily_Carr,_1936,_oil_on_paper.jpg

Cumulative amount: this resource includes ten distinct works by this artist, which is more extensive than a single classroom example and weighs against fairness; the justification depends on the separate, substantive comparison and criticism attached to each work, rather than a claim that a ten-image gallery is automatically permitted.

- Credit: TravelPhotosNL; artwork by Jeff Koons

- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0

- description: A bright green cat with purple eyes sits against a pale background. Loose black outlines describe its ears, paws and whiskers, while areas of colour do not try to imitate realistic fur. What personality do the uneven lines and unexpected colours give this cat?

## Bird study: female partridge

- File: assets/school-reviewed/28-hospice-and-lighthouse-honfleur.webp

- Rights: Public domain

- rightsAssessment: {"purpose": "Teacher-curated Canadian Grade7/8 art education and work-specific criticism, paired with original visual analysis and a discussion question.", "character": "One locally stored image per distinct artwork, at most640px; public website and GitHub distribution are acknowledged to be broader and more persistent than a closed classroom. - description: Donald Duck and Mickey Mouse appear in flat colours and heavy outlines borrowed from popular illustration. How does enlarging a familiar comic scene change what we notice about its shapes and humour?
- Information sources: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Look_Mickey

- description: A red sun rises between bands of cloud, dark land and radiating yellow rays. Thick outlines, flat colours and dotted areas give this landscape the bold clarity of a printed comic. Which repeated shapes make the sunlight feel as if it is spreading across the whole image?
- Information sources: https://www.nga.gov/artworks/76401-sunrise · https://www.wikiart.org/en/roy-lichtenstein/all-works

## Modern Head
- File: assets/gallery-modern/29-20728945.webp
- Credit: Photograph Yair Talmor; artwork Roy Lichtenstein
- Rights: CC BY-SA 3.0
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Modern_head_Daniel_garden.JPG
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; full photograph preserved.
- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/3.0
- rightsBasis: Photograph CC BY-SA3.0; permanently installed outdoor sculpture in Daniel Garden, Jerusalem, covered by Israeli freedom of panorama. Collection and location are documented in Commons artwork record and companion file JerusalemMunicipalityP4190022.JPG.
- description: Curved profiles, circular forms and straight metal edges combine into a tall, open construction that suggests a human head. Much of the face is created by empty space, so trees, sky and the viewer’s position become part of its changing appearance.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Modern_head_Daniel_garden.JPG

## Barcelona Head (El Cap de Barcelona)
- File: assets/extra/29-barcelona-head.webp
- Credit: Enfo · CC BY-SA 3.0 es
- Rights: CC BY-SA 3.0 es
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:73_Cap_de_Barcelona,_de_Roy_Lichtenstein.jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; content not cropped or altered.
- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/3.0/es/deed.en
- rightsBasis: Photographer CC BY-SA 3.0 Spain. The source file explicitly carries FoP-Spain and identifies the artwork in Barcelona’s public-art catalogue; underlying artwork is not claimed public domain.
- description: A towering face emerges from loops, dots and boldly edged coloured surfaces. The head can be recognised without realistic anatomy, while its varied patterns translate the graphic energy of Lichtenstein’s paintings into a large public sculpture.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:73_Cap_de_Barcelona,_de_Roy_Lichtenstein.jpg

## Brushstroke
- File: assets/gallery-modern/29-75843182.webp
- Credit: Tomás Fano; artwork by Roy Lichtenstein
- Rights: CC BY-SA 2.0
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Madrid._Roy_Lichtenstein_sculpture._The_Reina_Sof%C3%ADa_Museum._MNCARS._Spain_(2852266625).jpg
- Changes: Resized and converted to WebP; full photograph preserved.
- licenseUrl: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/2.0
- rightsBasis: Photograph CC BY-SA2.0; permanently displayed public courtyard sculpture in Spain, covered by Spanish freedom of panorama Article35.2. Museum record documents acquisition2006 and installation in the Jean Nouvel courtyard: https://www.museoreinasofia.es/colecciones/obra/brushstroke-pincelada/
- description: A huge curling mark rises through the museum courtyard, turning the trace of a quick brush movement into a solid sculpture. Its bold contours preserve the appearance of painted gesture while the rigid aluminium holds that gesture permanently in place.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Madrid._Roy_Lichtenstein_sculpture._The_Reina_Sof%C3%ADa_Museum._MNCARS._Spain_(2852266625).jpg · https://www.museoreinasofia.es/colecciones/obra/brushstroke-pincelada

- description: This wooden model translates a proposed church facade into three dimensions. Columns, empty niches and a central triangular pediment form a balanced architectural design. - description: Repeated window frames and a projecting roof cornice organise this Roman palace facade. Michelangelo continued the project after Antonio da Sangallo the Younger, contributing especially to the upper facade and cornice. The building is a collaboration across several architects, rather than a palace made by Michelangelo alone.

This is a contextual fair-dealing assessment, not an open licence or a conclusion that all educational copying is fair.
- rightsAssessment: Purpose: Canadian Grade 7/8 teaching and criticism of American People Series #4: The Civil Rights Triangle. Character: curated student resource, but publicly accessible GitHub Pages distribution increases reach and weighs against fairness compared with a restricted classroom. Amount: one whole creative work, necessary to discuss its composition and the specific visual relationships described here; no alternative crops or duplicate views are supplied. Alternatives: a text-only account would prevent students examining the evidence; openly licensed substitutes would not illustrate this particular work. Nature: a previously published artistic work, with copyright retained. Effect: at-most-640px display and integrated commentary reduce substitution for prints, licensed high-resolution files or museum visits, but possible licensing-market effects cannot be ruled out. Retain attribution, review if distribution or purpose changes, and respond to rights-holder concerns. Specific criticism focus: Differences in direction and expression make the group feel connected but not uniform. Whose gaze seems to guide your eye through the triangle? Source availability does not establish rights-holder permission; no claim is made that an unrelated website’s US fair-use rationale applies in Canada. Cumulative amount: this resource includes ten distinct works by this artist, which is more extensive than a single classroom example and weighs against fairness; the justification depends on the separate, substantive comparison and criticism attached to each work, rather than a claim that a ten-image gallery is automatically permitted.
- description: Differences in direction and expression make the group feel connected but not uniform. Whose gaze seems to guide your eye through the triangle?

- Rights: Public domain
- Source: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Tsunami_by_hokusai_19th_century.jpg

- description: A curling wave towers above long boats, with distant Mount Fuji appearing beneath the arch of water. The claw-like foam and repeating curves make the sea seem active, while the small, steady mountain creates a strong contrast in scale and movement.

## Ejiri in the Suruga province

- description: A smiling figure stretches its arms outward, its body divided into bright stripes, stars and other simple patterns. The open pose makes the greeting readable from a distance, while the painted metal brings the flat shapes of an illustration into a sculpture garden.
- Information sources: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:PikiWiki_Israel_15568_quot;Wellcomequot;_by_Romero_Britto_in_the_sculp.JPG

- rightsAssessment: Purpose: genuine classroom visual criticism with original commentary and questions, not decoration or sale. Character: teacher-curated study on a public GitHub Pages site and public repository, so exposure exceeds a closed class; this weighs against fairness and is acknowledged. Amount: one whole work image is necessary to discuss composition and relationships; ten distinct works are a limited selection from this artist’s larger output, each capped at 640pixels. Alternatives: text alone and substitute pictures cannot support criticism of this exact work; a closed classroom platform would reduce dissemination. Nature: already lawfully published museum/artist/Wikipedia illustration. Effect: small study image, original acknowledgement and no high-resolution download intended to avoid replacing prints, books or licensed high-quality reproductions; market for reproduction licences still exists and remains a countervailing factor. Assessment is contextual, not a guarantee or ownership claim.
- description: An angular figure and clustered objects press into a narrow painted space. Which features anticipate the dense arrangements of Pollock’s later abstractions?
- Information sources: https://americanart.si.edu/artwork/peddler-19822

Amount: one whole creative work, necessary to discuss its composition and the specific visual relationships described here; no alternative crops or duplicate views are supplied. Alternatives: a text-only account would prevent students examining the evidence; openly licensed substitutes would not illustrate this particular work. Nature: a previously published artistic work, with copyright retained. Effect: at-most-640px display and integrated commentary reduce substitution for prints, licensed high-resolution files or museum visits, but possible licensing-market effects cannot be ruled out. Retain attribution, review if distribution or purpose changes, and respond to rights-holder concerns. Specific criticism focus: Reducing the image to a record-like disc makes empty space carry unusual emphasis. How does the circular shape change your expectation of what the painting might sound like? Source availability does not establish rights-holder permission; no claim is made that an unrelated website’s US fair-use rationale applies in Canada. Cumulative amount: this resource includes ten distinct works by this artist, which is more extensive than a single classroom example and weighs against fairness; the justification depends on the separate, substantive comparison and criticism attached to each work, rather than a claim that a ten-image gallery is automatically permitted.
- description: Reducing the image to a record-like disc makes empty space carry unusual emphasis. How does the circular shape change your expectation of what the painting might sound like?
- Information sources: https://www.wikiart.org/en/jean-michel-basquiat/now-s-the-time
